- **productize**: Turn services into scalable products and build an offer ladder with upsells and downsells. - **profiling**: Use when chasing CPU performance problems in the running rapla JVM — slow queries, slow controllers, hot loops, framework overhead. Skip when targeted regression tests + log timing would be cheaper — profiling is for "where does the time actually go?" ([rapla/rapla](https://github.com/rapla/rapla/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/profiling) · ★71)
- **profiling**: 性能分析 ([chaterm/terminal-skills](https://github.com/chaterm/terminal-skills/tree/HEAD/performance/profiling) · ★58 · 2 copies)
- **profiling**: Evidence-first performance profiling — tool selection, methodology, interpretation. Use BEFORE guessing at performance issues. Tools first, logs second, code last. Covers: slow queries, high latency, CPU spikes, memory growth, throughput regressions (including 'got slower after deploy' — measure before assuming the deploy diff is the cause). Not for: crashes / logic errors (→ debug), tests being slow as test design issue (→ test-strategy). ([cookys/autopilot](https://github.com/cookys/autopilot/tree/HEAD/platforms/codex/plugin/skills/profiling) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **profiling**: 데이터 프로파일링 및 자동화된 EDA를 위한 유틸리티 스크립트 ([dandacompany/dantelabs-agentic-school](https://github.com/dandacompany/dantelabs-agentic-school/tree/HEAD/plugins/data-science/data-profiling/skills/profiling) · ★10)
- **profiling**: Finds where time, memory, and IO actually go inside a running system, using CPU and memory profilers, flame graphs, and the right choice between sampling and instrumentation, so optimization targets the real hot path instead of intuition. Use this whenever the user asks what's slow inside a specific process, wants to read or generate a flame graph, suspects a memory leak, or is about to optimize code without evidence of where the time goes. For deciding what to change once the hot path is known use `performance-tuning`, and for generating load to profile under use `load-testing`. ([arjunprabhulal/devops-skills](https://github.com/arjunprabhulal/devops-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/performance/profiling) · ★5)
- **profiling**: Capture and read an automated performance profile of the drawing app (web, Android, iOS). Use when measuring drawing/canvas performance, investigating jank or a slow interaction, verifying a perf change, or checking for regressions over time. Covers the `npm run perf:*` harness, how to read report.md/summary.json, and the bottleneck decision guide. ([KyleMit/Splotch](https://github.com/KyleMit/Splotch/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/profiling) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **profiling**: How to profile CPU, heap, and bundle size in this repo with Bun's markdown-format profilers. Use when a route or script is slow, memory grows without an obvious owner, the client bundle got bigger, or a performance claim needs a measurement before a rewrite. ([alexpricedev/Billet](https://github.com/alexpricedev/Billet/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/profiling) · ★3)
- **profiling**: Find where CPU, memory, and lock contention actually go in production. Reach for this when p99 spikes with no obvious cause, a service "got slow" after a deploy, GC pauses balloon, threads sit blocked, "the loop is hot but I don't know which line", a Python/Ruby worker pegs one core, or a JVM service shows healthy CPU but terrible throughput.
